## 2026-09-12 (midday, desk) — the doc-rot review, and its apply pass, on the whole repo

**Setup.** WSL, no hardware. Three peer sessions live (brx-fixes on the game-test M1/M2 in the main tree; brx-backhaul on
its own worktree/PR; brx-dry starting a contract generator). Method written first as `.claude/skills/doc-rot-review/SKILL.md`.

**Review.** Eleven read-only lanes, disjoint file sets, judged against what each file cites (routes vs `api.py`, fields vs
`types.py`/`types.ts`, runbook flags vs argparse, retractions vs the log). 625 tracked files, ~250 findings before dedupe,
~200 after subtracting the peers' in-flight work; ~55 P1. Ledger: artifact "Open BRX Rot Ledger". The nine cross-lane
shapes: a retraction lands in one file of a pair (fn 24, `$GLED` gate 5, the shield pool, the F23 formula, the 2166-id
bank); status re-grown in nine non-status files, always behind the code; three "GENERATED" headers on hand-kept copies;
the typed UI boundary drifted on the newest work and the contract test pinned 8 of ~18 shapes; three rules engines all
maintained; the amendment index used as a design inbox; guards reading HEAD not the tree; first-contact files the most
rotten while the deep files were excellent; 1.4 MB of bounded dead weight. The Python package had 7 dead one-liners in ~900.

**Apply.** Eight lanes, each with an exclusive file list and the peers' dirty set off-limits, every lane forbidden to run
repo scripts or touch the index; the main session committed each with `git commit --only`: 5e4ced8 c551196 27ba42c e4073f8
d02f027 f91b847 3e2eb36 94ca492 f356c33 edeb0d3, then this close on top of brx-fixes' M2 (7f1d6fd). What the lanes found the
REVIEW had wrong: `platform.md`'s "objective time" claim is true (`engine.js` emits `possession` with `hold_ms`); the two
facts called exclusive to the reference guides were already in the manual; `f11_ab.py` was a library for 18 scripts, not a
one-off (hoisted before deletion); three "closed" scripts were still named by live sheets (kept); the `resume()` live-relink
finding was fixed by M2 before this close. **Tests at the last full run before M2:** `mcp` 1367 passed / 2 failed (both M2's,
since fixed there) / 58 skipped; `webapp/mc` 207/207; `app` 321/321; `cd site && npm test` 90/90. **Conclusion:** the repo's
guards only ever caught what had broken once; the new ones encode every class this pass found, and the skill carries the
method plus the apply-phase lessons (exclusive ownership per lane, the peer's dirty set is authoritative, `git add` before
`--only` for new files, grep imports before deleting a "one-off").

**Polish loop over the apply commits (afternoon).** Three review lenses (doc accuracy, code/tests, rot regression) on
Opus, three rounds, fixes on Sonnet lanes with exclusive files, ten more `--only` commits (f557381 .. 20877d8). Round 1:
no Critical; five High (perks still hand-copied thrice; a living bench sheet sending the reader into the archive; the
stage-coverage guard blind to getters; a dangling cross-reference to the deleted polarity caveat; the friendly-fire rule
stated flat while F49 is unexplained) and ~15 Medium. Round 2: the §7 rewrite had deleted the utility spec's one
hardware-verified line; dev.md still stated friendly-fire flat; the unknown-module scan skipped modes/ and diag/; the
engine-method slice took the last brace. Round 3: three wording Mediums and one High (the modes/diag hole), all fixed.
What the loop taught: a cleanup pass writes its own rot (dated breadcrumbs, "GENERATED" claims that outrun the generator,
a hedge repeated four times), and a guard added in a hurry needs a can-fail test the same day. Final: `mcp`
1469 passed / 0 failed, `webapp/mc` 287/287, `app` 381/381, `cd site && npm test` 90/90 (after brx-fixes fixed the
12j locator its REVIEW KIT change had broken). Deferred Lows are in the round reports, not rows: `test_site_shots`
carries two overlapping staleness checks; the `[[slug]]` guard does not exempt fenced code; `_CITED_ID_ALLOW` keys on
basename; `if TYPE_CHECKING:` imports classify as eager; `pyproject` lists `packages = ["brx_mcp"]` only, which a wheel
would ship without subpackages; `backend.ts:500` filters `!k.hidden` over an already-filtered list.


## 2026-09-12 (keyboard) — A28 BACKHAUL: a phone with a data plan reaches MC off the field Wi-Fi, no per-phone setup (branch `backhaul-a28`, B30)

**Decision (Tony).** Not a requirement. A phone that has a data plan should keep getting game updates and kill
confirms when it wanders out of MC's Wi-Fi; a whole lobby on backhaul can unlock modes. Must work for anyone who
cloned the repo with **no custom setup on each phone** → cloudflared *quick tunnel* on the MC machine (no account,
no domain, no login), exposing the NODE SOCKET PORT only. Internet at the laptop is the one new field requirement,
and only when the host turns it on. Tony's step-0 idea became a REACH block on the Armory join card; the
"before any phones connect" constraint disappeared because MC pushes the public URL to already-joined phones.

**Contract:** contracts §5d + A28 index row (A26/A27 taken by uncommitted 2026-09-11 work; A29–A31 too, so A28 it is),
API.md (`lan.public`, `coverage`, `NodeView.reach`, `POST /api/tunnel`), FOLLOWUPS B30, ADR-0002 amendment note.
Key calls: the node **prefers backhaul** when offered (the only way coverage can be observed rather than asserted);
the join secret is enforced only on hellos that arrive through the tunnel or from a non-private peer; derived
coverage makes frag/survival ends authoritative but **never** lifts `time_limit_s`; `PARAMS.requires_coverage`
reserved, no catalog mode uses it.

**Build:** contract commit first, then three lanes on a worktree with disjoint files (server opus · MC UI sonnet ·
app transport sonnet), then a three-pass polish loop (correctness + security opus, UX sonnet with a real browser).
Passes closed **1 high + 13 medium**: the hello-based pub probe collided with the server's one-socket takeover
(now a reachability probe with no hello); app.js wiped the transport's persisted pub on every connect; pub/secret
were global, so a phone dialled the previous MC's tunnel with its key (now url- and session-scoped); the secret gate
keyed on a status that could flip to error while cloudflared still routed (now on the child being alive, plus an
orphan pid file, reap-with-escalation and a latch that keeps the gate armed when the kill fails); `reach` was
client-asserted (now stamped by MC from the socket path, and cleared through one `_drop_socket` exit); the 8 s
giveup armed only on `onopen` so a blackholed pub stuck the phone offline; `ws://` public URLs to public hosts
rejected. Deferred (B30): per-peer pre-hello cap, forged Cf header counting toward coverage, `origins=`.

**Verified (software only):** mcp 1349 (venv) / 1284+59 skipped (system python) · app 295+1 skip · MC UI tsc clean,
146 unit, backhaul e2e in headless Chromium at 1280/393 · site build + 90-step gate · shots regenerated. MC smoke-run
from the worktree with `--public-url` showed the two-URL QR, the public block and coverage in `/api/state`.
**Not yet:** a REAL cloudflared (every tunnel test drives a Python stand-in) and any hardware. Bench gate = one Pixel,
Wi-Fi off, data on, scan the QR, join, take a kill, see KILL CONFIRMED (B30).

**Also today:** `docs/architecture-topology.md` gained §2.1 and an accuracy pass (the "one phone, never a whole match"
claims were stale: two phones have played three whole matches); the `/platform` landing gained a Topology section
("Three links. Only one has to hold.") with the six design captions and the four plain costs.

**Idea parked (Tony's question):** a kill confirm over the BLE advert — the victim's advert already carries alive/seq,
three player bytes are spare; put `killed_by` in the value byte while dead and let the shooter's phone credit itself
locally. Presentation only, victim-authoritative scoring unchanged. Not on this branch.

## 2026-09-12 (session brx-dry) — contract DRY phase 1: one machine source for the node↔MC wire

**Built.** The wire tables (kinds, required fields, event types, size caps, timing constants) and 18 shared
TypedDict shapes lived three times — `envelope.py`, `envelope.js`, `types.ts` — hand-kept in step by nobody
checking. A mechanical diff found them already apart: `possession` was a persisted event on the Python side
and absent from the phone's whitelist (`envelope.js`); `result` demanded six fields on the MC side and only
`match_id` on the node side — deliberate (A24: a result must reach the engine even short a field), but until
today an unwritten asymmetry rather than a rule. `mcp/tools/gen_contract.py` now renders
`webapp/mc/src/api/contract.gen.ts` and `app/src/transport/contract.gen.js` from `mcp/brx_mcp/mc/types.py` +
`envelope.py`; `mcp/tests/test_contract_generated.py` (no skip path) fails CI on drift, importable
under bare system python. `envelope.js` imports the four tables from the generated file and re-exports every
name it exported before (no importer changed); `types.ts` re-exports the generated shapes and declares none
of them itself. `_REQUIRED`/`_EVENT_REQUIRED` go public as `REQUIRED`/`EVENT_REQUIRED`.

**The ACCEPT_MIN decision.** The rule lives once, in `envelope.py`: `ACCEPT_MIN: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] =
{"result": ("match_id",)}`. `validate(env, direction="mc")` (the node receiving from MC) checks
`ACCEPT_MIN.get(kind, REQUIRED[kind])`; `direction="node"` (MC receiving) always checks the full
`REQUIRED[kind]`. This makes A24's guarantee — a result reaches the engine even missing a field — an
invariant the generator and a test assert, rather than a fact that lived only in a comment on one side.

**The concurrency lesson.** Mid-lane, `types.py` picked up 139 lines that were not this lane's own work: a
`git commit --only <file>` from a peer session, sharing the one git index this repo's sessions all use, swept
the file's then-current state (someone else's app-0.2.0 `APP_MINOR` bump plus unrelated enrichments) into
`be1ce39` alongside the intended change. The content was correct — nothing was lost or wrong — but it landed
under a commit message that did not mention it. Lesson: when more than one session has the same file open,
diff what a `--only` commit is about to take, not just the path list, before trusting the message to describe
the diff.

**What stays hand-written (FOLLOWUPS F42.9–F42.13, filed today, none blocking):** the ~29 UI-only view types
(`State`, `LiveView`, `RecapView`, `NodeView`, `StationView`, `WeaponView`, …) have no Python TypedDict —
`api.py` builds them untyped, so there is nothing to generate from yet; pyright as a CI gate for `mcp/` is
unstarted (first manual run today: 249 errors, 81% of defs already annotated, the hot files are `state.py`
(79) and `stage/stage.py` (56)); `webapp/mc`'s `tsconfig` is not `strict` yet; `app/src/transport`
(`envelope.js`/`transport.js`) has no JSDoc + `checkJs` reading a generated `.d.ts`; `test_ui_contract.py`'s
DEFAULT_POLICY/voice test SKIPPED on an unparsable `gameSummary.ts` entry — a skip that had to be a parse
fix, not a permanent exemption (closed the same day by the polish pass below).

**Also found mid-session:** the working tree carried an unresolved merge (PR #3 `backhaul-a28` → `main`,
conflicts in `contracts.md`, `FOLLOWUPS.md`, this log, and several `mcp/brx_mcp/mc/*` + `app/src/*` files;
`types.py` briefly did not parse). It resolved (by another session) partway through this session's docs pass;
noted here because it blocked `python3 run_tests.py` for a stretch and is not a defect in the contract-DRY
work itself.

**Polish loop on `86a8c3a` (three read-only reviewers, then a fix pass, then two fresh reviewers):** no
Critical. One High: `test_ui_contract.py`'s DEFAULT_POLICY guard, the very test the commit message advertised,
ALWAYS skipped because its hand-rolled TS scanner could not read the `...over` spread in `gameSummary.ts`
(`_ts_object` now reads `...ident`; parser misses are AssertionErrors, never Skipped; 4/4, 0 skipped). Mediums,
all fixed: the generated file attached `PERSISTED_EVENT_TYPES`'s "this is a WHITELIST" comment to
`MAX_LOG_CHUNK_BYTES` (the comment rule is now ONLY "8+ spaces after `#` = continuation"; `FrameBundle.cues`
re-wrapped to that style); `envelope.py` still compared `shooter_num` against a literal 63 (now `MAX_PLAYERS`);
`_T_MIN_MS`/`_T_MAX_MS` were the last hand-mirrored pair (public, generated, imported by `envelope.js`; every
UPPER_CASE constant in `envelope.py` is now scanned generically); Literal aliases lost their comments. Lows
taken because they were one-liners in the same files: section dividers no longer become JSDoc, `STATION_SOURCE_IDS`
keeps source order, a non-`str` Literal member raises, the dead `MC_KINDS | {"apply"}` is gone, `Live.tsx`'s
hard-coded 8000 is `STALE_AFTER_MS`, the deleted `types.ts` nuance for `Loadout`/`LoadoutPool`/`LoadoutPolicy`
is back as `types.py` docstrings (comments travel). Found on the way: `test_site_shots.py`'s `_skip` called
`pytest.skip`, a BaseException `run_tests.py` does not catch, so under the venv a dirty UI tree ABORTED the
whole run with no summary line; it now raises the runner's Skipped unless `PYTEST_CURRENT_TEST` is set.

**Verified on the merged tree (`6925d32` + this pass):** `gen_contract.py --check` up to date; `run_tests.py`
1521 passed / 0 failed / 1 skipped under the venv, 1457 / 0 / 65 under system python (the one skip is
`test_site_shots` on a dirty tree, which regenerates with `cd site && npm run shots`, run this session);
`webapp/mc`: `tsc -b` clean, vitest 266/266; `app`: 365/365.

## 2026-09-12 (evening, desk) — the polish loop closed on milestone 2, and the merge with the backhaul branch

The M2 loop had stopped with round 2 half-applied (two lanes hit the account session limit). Finished it: the phone-side
round-2 review ran on the committed range and found a **Critical** — the A26 pick debounce held ONE pending pick per node,
so tapping a rifle on PRIMARY then a row on SECONDARY inside 400 ms destroyed the first pick silently — and a **High** of
my own making: `resume()` had been changed to reconcile in LIVE, and it fires on every `visibilitychange`, so a screen-off/
on wrote `$AMMO,0`, held the player disarmed for 3 s and re-armed from the SPAWN magazine (a free reload on demand). Both
fixed with fail-first tests (`commitPick` on slot switch/tab, `RESUME_GAP_MS` 5 s gate). The MC UI residuals (F129) landed
in the same commit (88b4d20). New residual: **F133** (the kit-lock latch can outlive a re-pushed game).

The backhaul branch (PR #3, 24 commits) was merged into this line (6925d32): 22 conflicts, all additive, kept both sides;
the branch had committed `app/node_modules` and `site/node_modules` as SELF-REFERENTIAL SYMLINKS from its worktree
(`.gitignore`'s `node_modules/` matches directories only), and checking them out replaced the real folders — dropped in
the merge, folders restored, a bare `node_modules` line added to .gitignore. Two shared-index lessons for the memory file:
`git commit --only <file>` commits the FILE's working copy (it swept a peer's 139 uncommitted lines once), and a peer's
untracked file under app/ blocks a clean APK sidecar.

**App 0.2.0** cut and published (release `app-v0.2.0`, from a clean 47a71bd) — the wire gained `result`, so the 0.x MINOR
moved on both sides at once (`types.APP_MINOR = 2`, pinned to app/package.json by a test). Final counts on the pushed tree:
python 1458/0 (once the mirror pin landed), app 381, MC UI 287, e2e:kit/e2e:m2/koth/ui:logsync green, HUD screen-truth
300/0 on the merged bundle, site shots regenerated on HEAD.
